================================================================================
FILE: .github\instructions\vocabulary-decks.instructions.md
================================================================================
---
description: "Use when creating or updating Japanese Estonian vocabulary or kanji flashcard decks from a pasted list. Covers source-faithful card data, back-only particle hints, original illustrations, Japanese text-to-speech, unique study storage, validation, and publishing."
---

# Japanese deck workflow

- Read [README.md](../../README.md) for the student-facing behavior and publishing overview.
- Use [chapter23_words.html](../../chapter23_words.html) as the template for a new words deck and [chapter23_kanji.html](../../chapter23_kanji.html) as the template for a new kanji deck.
- All layout, styling, UI rendering, and TTS logic live in [assets/styles.css](../../assets/styles.css) and [assets/deck.js](../../assets/deck.js). Each `chapter<N>_<type>.html` file only defines `<title>`, links `assets/styles.css`, sets `window.DECK_CONFIG`, and loads `assets/deck.js`.
- Create a separate root-level file named `chapter<N>_words.html` or `chapter<N>_kanji.html`. Update `<title>` and `DECK_CONFIG` (`chapter`, `type`, `heading`, `storageKey`, `orderStorageKey`, `cards`, `illustrations`). Leave existing decks untouched.

## Vocabulary and source fidelity

- Convert each supplied row into one `DECK_CONFIG.cards` entry in the supplied order. Keep IDs sequential starting at 1.
- Preserve the teacher-supplied Japanese form, kana reading, and Estonian wording exactly. Do not normalize spellings or readings.
- Ignore a separate English-translation column; do not put it in a card. If an English note is embedded in the supplied Estonian text, retain that source text rather than silently rewriting it.
- If the kanji field is blank, use its supplied kana form for both `kanji` and `reading`.
- For words and kanji decks, keep particle hints out of `estonian` and put them in the optional `particles` property so they appear only on the answer side below the reading. When a supplied Japanese form uses an argument placeholder directly with a particle (such as `〜を〜と結ぶ`), remove the complete placeholder-particle part from both `kanji` and `reading`, leaving the core Japanese form and its particle hint separate. Preserve ordinary Japanese particles that are part of a fixed expression, and keep the particle element hidden when there is no hint.
- Do not silently correct a supplied Estonian gloss. If a clarification is useful (or needed to disambiguate identical Estonian prompts within the same deck), append it in square brackets after the complete original gloss, for example `original wording [clarification]`.

## Illustrations and card behavior

- Add exactly one matching, original illustration to `DECK_CONFIG.illustrations` for every card, in the same order. Check that the actual symbols and accessible `description` convey the card's meaning, not merely a loosely related theme.
- Keep art self-contained as a meaning-matched emoji (`symbol` + optional `detail`) or inline SVG (`customSvg`). An emoji illustration must use an actual emoji symbol, never Japanese text, a kanji glyph, or a generic placeholder. Do not rely on a renderer fallback to provide an icon.
- Make the accessible `description` concise and specific to the card's meaning and illustration. Do not hotlink images or use copyrighted manga/anime artwork.
- Give each deck unique `storageKey` (`est_jp_flashcards_ch<N>_<type>_v1`) and `orderStorageKey` (`est_jp_flashcards_ch<N>_<type>_order_v1`) values so study progress remains independent.

## Validation and publishing

Before finishing:

1. Compare card count, order, Japanese forms/readings, and Estonian wording against the supplied list. Check sequential IDs, one meaning-matched illustration per card, accessible descriptions, actual emoji symbols (not text or placeholders) when using emoji, and particle hints absent from front-side meanings.
2. Run `python generate_index.py` from the repository root; it validates deck filenames, titles, `DECK_CONFIG` keys, card IDs, illustration counts, and speech targets before regenerating `index.html`. Do not edit `index.html` manually.
3. Commit and push only when the user asks to upload, publish, or otherwise requests it.

================================================================================
FILE: .github\prompts\create-flashcard-deck.prompt.md
================================================================================
---
description: "Create a new Japanese Estonian words or kanji flashcard deck"
name: "Create Flashcard Deck"
agent: "agent"
argument-hint: "Chapter number, deck type, and raw vocabulary or kanji list"
---

Create a new flashcard deck file named `chapter<N>_<words|kanji>.html` in the repository root from the raw list provided below.

Fill in these values before creating the file:

- `<N>`: chapter number
- `<words|kanji>`: deck type, either `words` or `kanji`
- `<heading>`: use `Chapter <N> Words · Estonian → Japanese` for words or `Chapter <N> Kanji · Estonian → Japanese` for kanji

Do NOT read or modify any existing `chapter*.html`, CSS, or JavaScript files. The repository instruction file [vocabulary-decks.instructions.md](../instructions/vocabulary-decks.instructions.md) defines source fidelity and validation rules. Follow this exact deck-file template directly:

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#fff1f7">
  <title>Chapter <N> <Words|Kanji> · Estonian to Japanese Flashcards</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="assets/styles.css">
</head>
<body>
  <script>
    window.DECK_CONFIG = {
      chapter: <N>,
      type: "<words|kanji>",
      heading: "<Chapter <N> Words | Estonian → Japanese · Chapter <N> Kanji>",
      storageKey: "est_jp_flashcards_ch<N>_<words|kanji>_v1",
      orderStorageKey: "est_jp_flashcards_ch<N>_<words|kanji>_order_v1",
      cards: [
        // Each card:
        // {
        //   id: 1,
        //   kanji: "...",
        //   reading: "...",
        //   estonian: "...",
        //   particles: "...", // optional; back side only
        //   speech: "..." // optional; Japanese ja-JP pronunciation override
        // }
      ],
      illustrations: [
        // One matching, original, accessible illustration object per card,
        // in exactly the same order as cards.
      ]
    };
  </script>
  <script src="assets/deck.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

Apply these rules:

1. Create only `chapter<N>_<words|kanji>.html` in the repository root.
2. Preserve source order and Japanese spelling/readings exactly.
3. If the supplied kanji field is blank, use the supplied kana for both `kanji` and `reading`.
4. Keep particle hints in optional `particles`, never in `estonian`.
5. Use an optional `speech` override for polyphonic or otherwise ambiguous readings when kana or normalization would produce the wrong pronunciation.
6. Keep Estonian wording unchanged unless a disambiguation is appended in square brackets.
7. Give every card exactly one meaning-matched illustration in `illustrations`, with an accessible description. Use an actual emoji symbol or inline SVG; never use Japanese text/kanji as the image, a generic fallback icon, copyrighted artwork, or hotlinked images.
8. Keep IDs sequential from 1.
9. Use the deck-specific storage keys shown in the template.
10. Validate card count, card order, ID sequence, illustration count, particle placement, and JavaScript syntax. Run `python generate_index.py` after creating the deck.

Raw deck list:

FILE: .github\workflows\pages.yml
================================================================================
name: Publish flashcard pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "*.html"
      - "assets/**"
      - "generate_index.py"
      - ".github/workflows/pages.yml"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: github-pages
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  deploy:
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Generate chapter index
        run: python3 generate_index.py --output-dir _site

      - name: Configure GitHub Pages
        uses: actions/configure-pages@v5

      - name: Upload website
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: "_site"

      - name: Deploy website
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4

      </section>
    </main>
  </div>`;

  document.getElementById("deck-heading").textContent = config.heading;

  let cards = INITIAL_CARDS.map((card) => ({ ...card, active: true, active: card.active !== false, inScope: true }));
  let currentIndex = 0;
  let isFlipped = false;
  let allowManualPreview = false;
  let randomOrderEnabled = false;
  let audioEnabled = false;
  let studyOrder = [];
  let previousRandomOrder = [];
  let randomStartId = INITIAL_CARDS[0].id;
  let randomEndId = INITIAL_CARDS[INITIAL_CARDS.length - 1].id;

  const cardList = document.getElementById("card-list");
  const activeCount = document.getElementById("active-count");
  const randomOrderToggle = document.getElementById("random-order-toggle");
  const audioToggle = document.getElementById("audio-toggle");
  const replayButton = document.getElementById("replay-button");
  const randomStartSelect = document.getElementById("random-start-card");
  const randomEndSelect = document.getElementById("random-end-card");
  const storageMessage = document.getElementById("storage-message");
  const cardPosition = document.getElementById("card-position");
  const mainStatus = document.getElementById("main-status");
  const frontContent = document.getElementById("front-content");
  const backContent = document.getElementById("back-content");
  const frontPrompt = document.getElementById("front-prompt");
  const frontIllustration = document.getElementById("front-illustration");
  const backPrompt = document.getElementById("back-prompt");
  const kanjiAnswer = document.getElementById("kanji-answer");
  const readingAnswer = document.getElementById("reading-answer");
  const particleAnswer = document.getElementById("particle-answer");

  const flipButton = document.getElementById("flip-button");
  const actions = document.getElementById("actions");
  const studyArea = document.getElementById("study-area");
  const completion = document.getElementById("completion");

  function createAnimeIllustration(card) {
    const illustration = ILLUSTRATIONS[card.id - 1];
    const hueOffset = IS_KANJI ? 335 : 28;
    const hue = ((card.id - 1) * 47 + hueOffset) % 360;
    const background = `hsl(${hue} 92% 94%)`;
    const accent = `hsl(${(hue + 40) % 360} 72% 74%)`;
    const rawLabel = `${card.estonian}: ${illustration.description}`;
    const label = rawLabel
      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
    const detail = illustration.detail
      ? `<text x="146" y="151" text-anchor="middle" font-size="34">${illustration.detail}</text>`
      : "";
    const mainImage = illustration.customSvg
      || `<text x="95" y="119" text-anchor="middle" font-size="76">${illustration.symbol}</text>${detail}`;

    if (IS_KANJI) {
      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 190 190" role="img" aria-label="${label}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
        <rect x="4" y="4" width="182" height="182" rx="30" fill="${background}"/>
        <path d="m28 39 3-7 3 7 7 3-7 3-3 7-3-7-7-3Zm128 108 2-5 2 5 5 2-5 2-2 5-2-5-5-2Z" fill="#fff" opacity=".95"/>
        <circle cx="41" cy="117" r="4" fill="${accent}"/>
        <circle cx="151" cy="52" r="5" fill="${accent}"/>
        <path d="M29 91c9-8 17-8 25 0m82 0c8-8 16-8 25 0" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".85"/>
        <circle cx="95" cy="95" r="63" fill="#fff" opacity=".68"/>
        ${mainImage}
        <path d="M63 157h64" stroke="${accent}" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".85"/>
      </svg>`;
    }

    return `<svg viewBox="0 0 190 190" role="img" aria-label="${label}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
      <rect x="4" y="4" width="182" height="182" rx="24" fill="${background}" stroke="#514352" stroke-width="4"/>
      <path d="M95 95 14 29m81 66L41 8m54 87L83 5m12 90 23-90m-23 90 55-82m-55 82 79-54M95 95l81-4m-81 4 78 47m-78-47 50 82m-50-82 7 88m-7-88-42 79m42-79-78 59m78-59-85 8" fill="none" stroke="#514352" stroke-width="2" opacity=".12"/>
      <path d="m28 39 3-7 3 7 7 3-7 3-3 7-3-7-7-3Zm128 108 2-5 2 5 5 2-5 2-2 5-2-5-5-2Z" fill="#fff" stroke="#514352" stroke-width="1.5" opacity=".95"/>
      <circle cx="41" cy="117" r="4" fill="${accent}"/>
      <circle cx="151" cy="52" r="5" fill="${accent}"/>
      <circle cx="95" cy="95" r="62" fill="#fff" opacity=".76" stroke="#514352" stroke-width="2.5"/>
      ${mainImage}
      <path d="M63 157h64" stroke="${accent}" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <path d="M12 12h24M154 178h24" stroke="#514352" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </svg>`;
  }

  function showStorageError(message) {
    storageMessage.textContent = message;
    storageMessage.hidden = false;
  }

  function loadProgress() {
    let saved;
    try {
      const value = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (value === null) return;
      saved = JSON.parse(value);
    } catch (error) {
      showStorageError(`Saved progress could not be loaded: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }

    const valid = Array.isArray(saved)
      && saved.length === INITIAL_CARDS.length
      && saved.every((item, index) => item
        && item.id === INITIAL_CARDS[index].id
        && typeof item.active === "boolean"
        && (item.inScope === undefined || typeof item.inScope === "boolean"));

    if (!valid) {
      showStorageError("Saved progress has an unexpected format. Starting with all cards active.");
      return;
    }

    cards = cards.map((card, index) => ({
      ...card,
      active: saved[index].active,
      inScope: saved[index].inScope !== false
    }));
    const initialActiveIndex = cards.findIndex((card) => card.inScope && card.active);
    if (initialActiveIndex !== -1) currentIndex = initialActiveIndex;
  }

  function saveOrderSettings() {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(ORDER_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
        enabled: randomOrderEnabled,
        startId: randomStartId,
        endId: randomEndId,
        previousOrder: previousRandomOrder
      }));
    } catch (error) {
      showStorageError(`Random order preference could not be saved: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  function createRandomOrder(selectFirst = true) {
    studyOrder = cards
      .map((card, index) => card.inScope && card.active
        && card.id >= randomStartId && card.id <= randomEndId ? index : -1)
      .filter((index) => index !== -1);

    for (let index = studyOrder.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
      const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
      [studyOrder[index], studyOrder[swapIndex]] = [studyOrder[swapIndex], studyOrder[index]];
    }

    const newOrder = studyOrder.map((index) => cards[index].id);
    const sameCards = newOrder.length === previousRandomOrder.length
      && newOrder.every((id) => previousRandomOrder.includes(id));
    const sameOrder = sameCards && newOrder.every((id, index) => id === previousRandomOrder[index]);

    if (sameOrder && studyOrder.length > 1) {
      studyOrder.push(studyOrder.shift());
    }

    if (studyOrder.length > 1 && studyOrder[0] === currentIndex) {
      [studyOrder[0], studyOrder[1]] = [studyOrder[1], studyOrder[0]];
    }

    previousRandomOrder = studyOrder.map((index) => cards[index].id);
    if (selectFirst && studyOrder.length > 0) currentIndex = studyOrder[0];
    saveOrderSettings();
  }

  function initializeRandomRangeSelectors() {
    for (const card of INITIAL_CARDS) {
      const label = `#${card.id} ${card.estonian}`;
      randomStartSelect.add(new Option(label, String(card.id)));
      randomEndSelect.add(new Option(label, String(card.id)));
    }

    randomStartSelect.value = String(randomStartId);
    randomEndSelect.value = String(randomEndId);
  }

  function appendEligibleCardsToRandomOrder() {
    studyOrder = studyOrder.filter((index) => cards[index].inScope && cards[index].active
      && cards[index].id >= randomStartId && cards[index].id <= randomEndId);
    const alreadyOrdered = new Set(studyOrder);
    const additions = cards
      .map((card, index) => card.inScope && card.active
        && card.id >= randomStartId && card.id <= randomEndId
        && !alreadyOrdered.has(index) ? index : -1)
      .filter((index) => index !== -1);

    for (let index = additions.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
      const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
      [additions[index], additions[swapIndex]] = [additions[swapIndex], additions[index]];
    }

    studyOrder.push(...additions);
  }

  function loadOrderSettings() {
    let saved;
    try {
      const value = window.localStorage.getItem(ORDER_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (value === null) return;
      saved = JSON.parse(value);
    } catch (error) {
      showStorageError(`Random order preference could not be loaded: ${error.message}`);
      return;
    }

    const savedStartId = saved?.startId ?? INITIAL_CARDS[0].id;
    const savedEndId = saved?.endId ?? INITIAL_CARDS[INITIAL_CARDS.length - 1].id;
    const valid = saved
      && typeof saved.enabled === "boolean"
      && Number.isInteger(savedStartId)
      && Number.isInteger(savedEndId)
      && savedStartId >= INITIAL_CARDS[0].id
      && savedEndId <= INITIAL_CARDS[INITIAL_CARDS.length - 1].id
      && savedStartId <= savedEndId
      && Array.isArray(saved.previousOrder)
      && saved.previousOrder.every((id) => INITIAL_CARDS.some((card) => card.id === id));
    if (!valid) {
      showStorageError("Saved random order preference has an unexpected format.");
      return;
    }

    randomOrderEnabled = saved.enabled;
    randomStartId = savedStartId;
    randomEndId = savedEndId;
    randomStartSelect.value = String(randomStartId);
    randomEndSelect.value = String(randomEndId);
    previousRandomOrder = saved.previousOrder;
    randomOrderToggle.checked = randomOrderEnabled;
    if (randomOrderEnabled) createRandomOrder();
  }

  function saveProgress() {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(
        STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify(cards.map(({ id, active, inScope }) => ({ id, active, inScope })))
      );
      storageMessage.hidden = true;
      storageMessage.textContent = "";
    } catch (error) {
      showStorageError(`Progress could not be saved: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  function buildSidebar() {
    cardList.replaceChildren(...cards.map((card, index) => {
      const row = document.createElement("li");
      row.className = "card-row";
      row.dataset.index = String(index);

      const select = document.createElement("button");
      select.className = "card-select";
      select.type = "button";
      select.setAttribute("aria-label", `Load card ${card.id}: ${card.estonian}`);

      const top = document.createElement("span");
      top.className = "card-row-top";
      const number = document.createElement("span");
      number.className = "card-number";
      number.textContent = `#${card.id}`;
      const badge = document.createElement("span");
      badge.className = "status-badge";
      const scopeBadge = document.createElement("span");
      scopeBadge.className = "scope-badge";
      top.append(number, badge, scopeBadge);

      const estonian = document.createElement("span");
      estonian.className = "card-estonian";
      estonian.textContent = card.estonian;
      select.append(top, estonian);

      const toggleStack = document.createElement("span");
      toggleStack.className = "toggle-stack";

      const activeControl = document.createElement("label");
      activeControl.className = "toggle-control active-toggle";
      activeControl.append(document.createTextNode("Study"));
      const activeToggle = document.createElement("input");
      activeToggle.type = "checkbox";
      activeToggle.dataset.index = String(index);
      activeToggle.dataset.kind = "active";
      const activeTrack = document.createElement("span");
      activeTrack.className = "switch-track";
      const activeLabel = document.createElement("span");
      activeLabel.className = "toggle-label";
      activeLabel.append(activeToggle, activeTrack);
      activeControl.append(activeLabel);

      const scopeControl = document.createElement("label");
      scopeControl.className = "toggle-control scope-toggle";
      scopeControl.append(document.createTextNode("Scope"));
      const scopeToggle = document.createElement("input");
      scopeToggle.type = "checkbox";
      scopeToggle.dataset.index = String(index);
      scopeToggle.dataset.kind = "scope";
      const scopeTrack = document.createElement("span");
      scopeTrack.className = "switch-track";
      const scopeLabel = document.createElement("span");
      scopeLabel.className = "toggle-label";
      scopeLabel.append(scopeToggle, scopeTrack);
      scopeControl.append(scopeLabel);

      toggleStack.append(activeControl, scopeControl);
      row.append(select, toggleStack);
      return row;
    }));
  }

  function updateSidebar(scrollIntoView = false) {
    const inScopeTotal = cards.filter((card) => card.inScope).length;
    const activeTotal = cards.filter((card) => card.inScope && card.active).length;
    activeCount.textContent = `Active: ${activeTotal} / ${inScopeTotal} · In scope: ${inScopeTotal} / ${cards.length}`;
    document.getElementById("reactivate-scoped-button").disabled = inScopeTotal === 0;

    for (let index = 0; index < cards.length; index += 1) {
      const card = cards[index];
      const row = cardList.children[index];
      const isCurrent = index === currentIndex;
      row.classList.toggle("is-inactive", !card.active);
      row.classList.toggle("is-out-of-scope", !card.inScope);
      row.classList.toggle("is-current", isCurrent);

      const select = row.querySelector(".card-select");
      select.setAttribute("aria-current", isCurrent ? "true" : "false");

      const badge = row.querySelector(".status-badge");
      badge.textContent = card.active ? "Active" : "Memorized";

      const scopeBadge = row.querySelector(".scope-badge");
      scopeBadge.textContent = card.inScope ? "In scope" : "Out of scope";
      scopeBadge.classList.toggle("is-out-of-scope", !card.inScope);

      const activeToggle = row.querySelector('input[data-kind="active"]');
      activeToggle.checked = card.active;
      activeToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", `${card.active ? "Deactivate" : "Reactivate"} card ${card.id}`);

      const scopeToggle = row.querySelector('input[data-kind="scope"]');
      scopeToggle.checked = card.inScope;
      scopeToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", `${card.inScope ? "Descope" : "Rescope"} card ${card.id}`);

      if (isCurrent && scrollIntoView) {
        const rowRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
        const listRect = cardList.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (rowRect.top < listRect.top) cardList.scrollTop += rowRect.top - listRect.top;
        if (rowRect.bottom > listRect.bottom) cardList.scrollTop += rowRect.bottom - listRect.bottom;
        if (rowRect.left < listRect.left) cardList.scrollLeft += rowRect.left - listRect.left;
        if (rowRect.right > listRect.right) cardList.scrollLeft += rowRect.right - listRect.right;
      }
    }
  }

  let japaneseVoice = null;

  function updateJapaneseVoice() {
    const synthesis = window.speechSynthesis;
    if (!synthesis) return;

    const preferredVoiceName = /kyoko|otoya|nanami|haruka|ichiro|google.*日本語/iu;
    const voiceScore = (voice) => (voice.localService ? 100 : 0)
      + (voice.lang.toLowerCase() === "ja-jp" ? 40 : 0)
      + (preferredVoiceName.test(voice.name) ? 20 : 0);
    japaneseVoice = synthesis.getVoices()
      .filter((voice) => /^ja(?:-|$)/iu.test(voice.lang))
      .sort((left, right) => voiceScore(right) - voiceScore(left))[0] || null;
  }

  if (typeof window.speechSynthesis !== "undefined") {
    updateJapaneseVoice();
    if (typeof window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener === "function") {
      window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener("voiceschanged", updateJapaneseVoice);
    } else {
      window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = updateJapaneseVoice;
    }
  }

  const SPEECH_OVERRIDES = {
    "放っておく": "ほうっておく",
    "表⇄裏(うら)": "おもて うら",
    "一歩・二歩": "いっぽ にほ",
    "宿": "やど",
    "角": "かど",
    "内": "うち"
  };

  function getJapaneseSpeechText(card) {
    if (typeof card.speech === "string" && card.speech.trim()) {
      return card.speech.trim();
    }
    const rawSource = typeof card.kanji === "string" && card.kanji.trim()
      ? card.kanji.trim()
      : card.reading;
    const source = SPEECH_OVERRIDES[rawSource] || rawSource;
    return source
      .split(/\bNB!/iu, 1)[0]
      .replace(/[(（](?:する|な|の)[)）]/gu, (match) => match.slice(1, -1))
      .replace(/(?<=[\p{Script=Han}\p{Script=Hiragana}\p{Script=Katakana}ー])[(（][^()（）]*[)）]/gu, "")
      .replace(/[()[\]{}（）［］｛｝]/gu, "")
      .replace(/[〜～~]/gu, "")
      .replace(/\bX\b/gu, "")
      .replace(/[\/／|・、，,;；⇄]/gu, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/gu, " ")
      .trim();
  }

  function speakJapanese(text) {
    if (!audioEnabled || typeof window.speechSynthesis === "undefined"
      || typeof SpeechSynthesisUtterance === "undefined") return;
    const spokenText = String(text || "").trim();
    if (!spokenText) return;

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(spokenText);
    utterance.lang = "ja-JP";
    utterance.rate = 0.95;
    updateJapaneseVoice();
    if (japaneseVoice) utterance.voice = japaneseVoice;

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  }

  function renderMain() {
    const activeInScope = cards.some((card) => card.inScope && card.active
      && (!randomOrderEnabled || (card.id >= randomStartId && card.id <= randomEndId)));
    const inScopeTotal = cards.filter((card) => card.inScope).length;
    const scopedInActivePool = cards.filter((card) => card.inScope
      && (!randomOrderEnabled || (card.id >= randomStartId && card.id <= randomEndId))).length;
    const showStudyArea = activeInScope || allowManualPreview;
    studyArea.hidden = !showStudyArea;
    completion.hidden = showStudyArea;
    document.getElementById("completion-message").textContent = inScopeTotal === 0
      ? "No cards are currently in scope. Use the Scope switches in the list to choose which cards you want to study."
      : randomOrderEnabled && !activeInScope
        ? `No active in-scope cards between #${randomStartId} and #${randomEndId}. Adjust the random range or reactivate scoped cards.`
        : "You have memorized every card in your study scope. Reactivate scoped cards whenever you are ready for another round.";
    document.getElementById("reactivate-button").hidden = scopedInActivePool === 0;
    if (!showStudyArea) return;

    const card = cards[currentIndex];
    cardPosition.textContent = `Card #${card.id} of ${cards.length}`;
    mainStatus.textContent = card.active ? "Active" : "Deactivated";
    mainStatus.className = `status-badge${card.active ? "" : " is-inactive"}`;
    if (!card.inScope) {
      mainStatus.textContent = "Out of scope";
      mainStatus.className = "scope-badge is-out-of-scope";
    }
    frontPrompt.textContent = card.estonian;
    backPrompt.textContent = card.estonian;
    kanjiAnswer.textContent = card.kanji;
    readingAnswer.textContent = card.reading;
    particleAnswer.textContent = card.particles || "";
    particleAnswer.hidden = !card.particles;
    const illustration = createAnimeIllustration(card);
    frontIllustration.innerHTML = illustration;
    answerIllustration.innerHTML = illustration;
    frontContent.hidden = isFlipped;
    backContent.hidden = !isFlipped;
    flipButton.hidden = isFlipped;
    actions.hidden = !isFlipped;
  }

  function render(scrollIntoView = false) {
    updateSidebar(scrollIntoView);
    renderMain();
  }

  function loadCard(index) {
    currentIndex = index;
    isFlipped = false;
    allowManualPreview = true;
    render(true);
  }

  function advanceToNextActive() {
    allowManualPreview = false;
    isFlipped = false;
    if (randomOrderEnabled) {
      appendEligibleCardsToRandomOrder();
      const orderPosition = studyOrder.indexOf(currentIndex);
      if (orderPosition === -1) {
        const nextEligibleIndex = studyOrder.find((index) => cards[index].inScope && cards[index].active);
        if (nextEligibleIndex !== undefined) {
          currentIndex = nextEligibleIndex;
          render(true);
          return;
        }
      } else {
        for (let nextPosition = orderPosition + 1; nextPosition < studyOrder.length; nextPosition += 1) {
          const nextIndex = studyOrder[nextPosition];
          if (cards[nextIndex].inScope && cards[nextIndex].active) {
            currentIndex = nextIndex;
            render(true);
            return;
          }
        }
      }

      createRandomOrder();
      render(true);
      return;
    }

    for (let offset = 1; offset <= cards.length; offset += 1) {
      const nextIndex = (currentIndex + offset) % cards.length;
      if (cards[nextIndex].inScope && cards[nextIndex].active) {
        currentIndex = nextIndex;
        render(true);
        return;
      }
    }
    render();
  }

  function resetAllCards() {
    cards = INITIAL_CARDS.map((card) => ({ ...card, active: true, inScope: true }));
    currentIndex = 0;
    isFlipped = false;
    allowManualPreview = false;
    saveProgress();
    if (randomOrderEnabled) createRandomOrder();
    render(true);
  }

  function reactivateScopedCards() {
    cards = cards.map((card) => ({
      ...card,
      active: card.inScope ? true : card.active
    }));
    currentIndex = cards.findIndex((card) => card.inScope);
    if (currentIndex === -1) currentIndex = 0;
    isFlipped = false;
    allowManualPreview = false;
    saveProgress();
    if (randomOrderEnabled) createRandomOrder();
    render(true);
  }

  cardList.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
    const select = event.target.closest(".card-select");
    if (!select) return;
    const row = select.closest(".card-row");
    select.blur();
    loadCard(Number(row.dataset.index));
  });

  cardList.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
    const toggle = event.target.closest('input[type="checkbox"]');
    if (!toggle) return;
    const index = Number(toggle.dataset.index);
    if (toggle.dataset.kind === "scope") {
      cards[index].inScope = toggle.checked;
    } else {
      cards[index].active = toggle.checked;
    }
    saveProgress();
    if (randomOrderEnabled) appendEligibleCardsToRandomOrder();
    render();
  });

  randomOrderToggle.addEventListener("change", () => {
    randomOrderEnabled = randomOrderToggle.checked;
    if (randomOrderEnabled) {
      createRandomOrder();
    } else {
      studyOrder = [];
      saveOrderSettings();
    }
    isFlipped = false;
    render(true);
  });

  randomStartSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
    randomStartId = Number(randomStartSelect.value);
    if (randomStartId > randomEndId) {
      randomEndId = randomStartId;
      randomEndSelect.value = String(randomEndId);
    }
    if (randomOrderEnabled) createRandomOrder();
    else saveOrderSettings();
    isFlipped = false;
    render(true);
  });

  randomEndSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
    randomEndId = Number(randomEndSelect.value);
    if (randomEndId < randomStartId) {
      randomStartId = randomEndId;
      randomStartSelect.value = String(randomStartId);
    }
    if (randomOrderEnabled) createRandomOrder();
    else saveOrderSettings();
    isFlipped = false;
    render(true);
  });

  document.getElementById("reset-button").addEventListener("click", resetAllCards);
  document.getElementById("reactivate-scoped-button").addEventListener("click", reactivateScopedCards);
  document.getElementById("reactivate-button").addEventListener("click", reactivateScopedCards);

  function revealCurrentCard() {
    if (isFlipped || studyArea.hidden) return;
    isFlipped = true;
    renderMain();
    speakJapanese(getJapaneseSpeechText(cards[currentIndex]));
  }

  flipButton.addEventListener("click", revealCurrentCard);
  audioToggle.addEventListener("change", () => {
    audioEnabled = audioToggle.checked;
    replayButton.disabled = !audioEnabled;
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(AUDIO_STORAGE_KEY, String(audioEnabled));
    } catch {}
    if (!audioEnabled && typeof window.speechSynthesis !== "undefined") {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
  });

  replayButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (isFlipped && !studyArea.hidden) {
      speakJapanese(getJapaneseSpeechText(cards[currentIndex]));
    }
  });

  document.getElementById("keep-button").addEventListener("click", () => {
    cards[currentIndex].active = true;
    cards[currentIndex].inScope = true;
    saveProgress();
    if (randomOrderEnabled) appendEligibleCardsToRandomOrder();
    advanceToNextActive();
  });

  document.getElementById("deactivate-button").addEventListener("click", () => {
    cards[currentIndex].active = false;
    saveProgress();
    advanceToNextActive();
  });

  window.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.target.matches("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable='true']")) return;
    if (event.key.toLowerCase() === "r" && isFlipped && !studyArea.hidden) {
      event.preventDefault();
      speakJapanese(getJapaneseSpeechText(cards[currentIndex]));
      return;
    }
    if (event.target.matches("button") || studyArea.hidden) return;

    if (event.code === "Space" && !isFlipped) {
      event.preventDefault();
      revealCurrentCard();
    } else if (isFlipped && (event.key === "1" || event.key === "ArrowRight")) {
      event.preventDefault();
      document.getElementById("keep-button").click();
    } else if (isFlipped && (event.key === "2" || event.key === "Enter")) {
      event.preventDefault();
      document.getElementById("deactivate-button").click();
    }
  });

  buildSidebar();
  initializeRandomRangeSelectors();
  loadProgress();
  loadOrderSettings();
  try {
    audioEnabled = window.localStorage.getItem(AUDIO_STORAGE_KEY) === "true";
  } catch {}
  audioToggle.checked = audioEnabled;
  replayButton.disabled = !audioEnabled;
  render(true);
})();

FILE: generate_index.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import html
import re
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import quote


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
DECK_FILENAME_PATTERN = re.compile(r"chapter(\d+)_(kanji|words)\.html")


def extract_config_array(source: str, key: str) -> str | None:
    match = re.search(
        rf"^[ \t]*{re.escape(key)}:\s*\[(.*?)^[ \t]*\],?",
        source,
        re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL,
    )
    return match.group(1) if match else None


def japanese_speech_target(source: str) -> str:
    target = source.split("NB!", maxsplit=1)[0]
    target = re.sub(r"[(（](?:する|な|の)[)）]", lambda match: match.group()[1:-1], target)
    target = re.sub(
        r"(?<=[一-龯々〆ヶぁ-ゖァ-ヺー])[(（][^()（）]*[)）]",
        "",
        target,
    )
    target = re.sub(r"[()[\]{}（）［］｛｝〜～~]", "", target)
    target = re.sub(r"\bX\b", "", target)
    target = re.sub(r"[/／|・、，,;；⇄]", " ", target)
    return " ".join(target.split())


def validate_decks(pages: list[Path]) -> None:
    errors: list[str] = []
    seen_storage_keys: dict[str, Path] = {}

    if not (ROOT / "assets" / "styles.css").is_file():
        errors.append("assets/styles.css is missing")
    if not (ROOT / "assets" / "deck.js").is_file():
        errors.append("assets/deck.js is missing")

    for page in pages:
        filename_match = DECK_FILENAME_PATTERN.fullmatch(page.name)
        if not filename_match:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: filename must match chapter<N>_(kanji|words).html")
            continue

        chapter, deck_type = filename_match.groups()
        source = page.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        expected_title = f"Chapter {chapter} {deck_type.title()} · Estonian to Japanese Flashcards"
        expected_heading = (
            f"Estonian → Japanese · Chapter {chapter} Kanji"
            if deck_type == "kanji"
            else f"Chapter {chapter} Words"
        )

        title_match = re.search(r"<title>(.*?)</title>", source, re.DOTALL)
        if not title_match or html.unescape(title_match.group(1).strip()) != expected_title:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: <title> must be {expected_title!r}")

        if 'href="assets/styles.css"' not in source:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: must link assets/styles.css")
        if 'src="assets/deck.js"' not in source:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: must load assets/deck.js")

        type_match = re.search(r'\btype:\s*"([^"]*)"', source)
        if not type_match or type_match.group(1) != deck_type:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: DECK_CONFIG.type must be {deck_type!r}")

        heading_match = re.search(r'\bheading:\s*"([^"]*)"', source)
        if not heading_match or html.unescape(heading_match.group(1).strip()) != expected_heading:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: DECK_CONFIG.heading must be {expected_heading!r}")

        expected_storage_keys = {
            "storageKey": f"est_jp_flashcards_ch{chapter}_{deck_type}_v1",
            "orderStorageKey": f"est_jp_flashcards_ch{chapter}_{deck_type}_order_v1",
        }
        for key_name, expected_key in expected_storage_keys.items():
            key_match = re.search(
                rf'\b{key_name}:\s*"([^"]*)"',
                source,
            )
            if not key_match or key_match.group(1) != expected_key:
                errors.append(f"{page.name}: {key_name} must be {expected_key!r}")
                continue
            previous_page = seen_storage_keys.get(expected_key)
            if previous_page:
                errors.append(
                    f"{page.name}: storage key {expected_key!r} is also used by {previous_page.name}"
                )
            else:
                seen_storage_keys[expected_key] = page

        cards = extract_config_array(source, "cards")
        illustrations = extract_config_array(source, "illustrations")
        if cards is None:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: DECK_CONFIG.cards array was not found")
        if illustrations is None:
            errors.append(f"{page.name}: DECK_CONFIG.illustrations array was not found")
        if cards is not None and illustrations is not None:
            card_ids = [
                int(card_id)
                for card_id in re.findall(r"^\s*\{\s*id:\s*(\d+)\b", cards, re.MULTILINE)
            ]
            card_matches = list(re.finditer(
                r'^\s*\{\s*id:\s*(\d+)\b[^\r\n]*?\bkanji:\s*"((?:\\.|[^"\\])*)"',
                cards,
                re.MULTILINE,
            ))
            if len(card_matches) != len(card_ids):
                errors.append(f"{page.name}: every card must have a kanji speech target")
            for card_match in card_matches:
                card_id, kanji = card_match.groups()
                if not japanese_speech_target(kanji):
                    errors.append(
                        f"{page.name}: card {card_id} kanji has no speech text after cleanup"
                    )
            illustration_count = len(
                re.findall(
                    r"^[ \t]*\{",
                    illustrations,
                    re.MULTILINE,
                )
            )
            expected_ids = list(range(1, len(card_ids) + 1))
            if card_ids != expected_ids:
                errors.append(f"{page.name}: card ids must be sequential from 1")
            if len(card_ids) != illustration_count:
                errors.append(
                    f"{page.name}: {len(card_ids)} cards but {illustration_count} illustrations"
                )

    deck_js_path = ROOT / "assets" / "deck.js"
    if deck_js_path.is_file():
        deck_js = deck_js_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if "card.kanji" not in deck_js or "⇄" not in deck_js:
            errors.append("assets/deck.js: getJapaneseSpeechText must use card.kanji and include ⇄")

    if errors:
        print("Deck validation failed:", file=sys.stderr)
        for error in errors:
            print(f"- {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)


def natural_key(value: str) -> list[int | str]:
    return [int(part) if part.isdigit() else part.casefold()
            for part in re.split(r"(\d+)", value)]


def page_title(path: Path) -> str:
    chapter = re.fullmatch(r"chapter[-_ ]?(\d+)(?:_(kanji|words))?", path.stem, re.IGNORECASE)
    if chapter:
        suffix = f" {chapter.group(2).title()}" if chapter.group(2) else ""
        return f"Chapter {chapter.group(1)}{suffix}"
    return path.stem.replace("-", " ").replace("_", " ").title()


def chapter_pages() -> list[Path]:
    return sorted(
        (path for path in ROOT.glob("*.html") if path.name.casefold() != "index.html"),
        key=lambda path: natural_key(path.name),
    )


def build_index(pages: list[Path]) -> str:
    page_links = "\n".join(
        f'        <a class="chapter" href="{quote(path.name)}">'
        f'<span>{html.escape(page_title(path))}</span>'
        '<span class="arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span></a>'
        for path in pages
    )
    empty_message = (
        ""
        if pages
        else '<p class="empty">Add HTML flashcard pages to this repository to list them here.</p>'
    )

    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#fff1f7">
  <title>Japanese Flashcards · Chapters</title>
  <style>
    :root {{
      color-scheme: light;
      font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
      color: #3b3044;
      background: #fff1f7;
      font-synthesis: none;
      text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
    }}
    * {{ box-sizing: border-box; }}
    body {{
      display: grid;
      min-height: 100vh;
      min-height: 100dvh;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 28px 18px;
      place-items: center;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 12% 18%, #f8d8e8 0 2px, transparent 3px),
        radial-gradient(circle at 88% 76%, #e8defa 0 3px, transparent 4px),
        radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 42%, #fff 0, #fff8fc 48%, #f9eaf4 100%);
    }}
    main {{
      width: min(100%, 560px);
      padding: clamp(24px, 6vw, 44px);
      border: 2px solid #f0d5e4;
      border-radius: 28px;
      background: #fff;
      box-shadow: 0 18px 55px #743b5a15;
    }}
    h1 {{ margin: 0; font-size: clamp(1.7rem, 6vw, 2.3rem); }}
    .intro {{ margin: 10px 0 24px; color: #817588; line-height: 1.5; }}
    nav {{ display: grid; gap: 10px; }}
    .chapter {{
      display: flex;
      min-height: 58px;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 16px;
      padding: 14px 17px;
      border: 1px solid #f0dce8;
      border-radius: 14px;
      background: #fffafd;
      color: #554258;
      font-weight: 700;
      text-decoration: none;
      transition: border-color 130ms ease, transform 130ms ease, background 130ms ease;
    }}
    .chapter:hover {{ transform: translateY(-2px); border-color: #e595b8; background: #fff0f6; }}
    .chapter:focus-visible {{ outline: 3px solid #eca5c3; outline-offset: 3px; }}
    .arrow {{ color: #b94178; font-size: 1.3rem; }}
    .empty {{ color: #817588; }}
    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {{
      *, *::before, *::after {{ scroll-behavior: auto !important; transition-duration: 0.01ms !important; }}
    }}
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main>
    <h1>Japanese Flashcards</h1>
    <p class="intro">Choose a chapter to start studying.</p>
    <nav aria-label="Flashcard chapters">
{page_links}
    </nav>
{empty_message}
  </main>
</body>
</html>
"""


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate an index of the repository's HTML pages.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=ROOT,
        help="Output directory (HTML pages and assets are copied here alongside the generated index).",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    pages = chapter_pages()
    validate_decks(pages)
    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if args.output_dir.resolve() != ROOT:
        shutil.copytree(ROOT / "assets", args.output_dir / "assets", dirs_exist_ok=True)
        for page in pages:
            shutil.copy2(page, args.output_dir / page.name)
    (args.output_dir / "index.html").write_bytes(build_index(pages).encode("utf-8"))
    print(f"Generated index.html with {len(pages)} chapter page(s) in {args.output_dir}.")

FILE: README.md

# Japanese flashcards

This repository contains responsive, standalone HTML flashcard decks for
studying Japanese from Estonian. Each deck includes its own card data,
illustrations, study controls, and saved progress; shared styling and study logic live in assets/styles.css and assets/deck.js, with no third-party dependencies.

## Current decks

| Deck | File |
| --- | --- |
| Chapter 17 Kanji | [chapter17_kanji.html](./chapter17_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 17 Words | [chapter17_words.html](./chapter17_words.html) |
| Chapter 18 Kanji | [chapter18_kanji.html](./chapter18_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 18 Words | [chapter18_words.html](./chapter18_words.html) |
| Chapter 19 Kanji | [chapter19_kanji.html](./chapter19_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 19 Words | [chapter19_words.html](./chapter19_words.html) |
| Chapter 20 Kanji | [chapter20_kanji.html](./chapter20_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 20 Words | [chapter20_words.html](./chapter20_words.html) |
| Chapter 21 Kanji | [chapter21_kanji.html](./chapter21_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 21 Words | [chapter21_words.html](./chapter21_words.html) |
| Chapter 22 Kanji | [chapter22_kanji.html](./chapter22_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 22 Words | [chapter22_words.html](./chapter22_words.html) |
| Chapter 23 Kanji | [chapter23_kanji.html](./chapter23_kanji.html) |
| Chapter 23 Words | [chapter23_words.html](./chapter23_words.html) |

## Studying

- The front shows the Estonian meaning. Flip with the cat button labelled
  フリップ or press **Space**; the back shows the Japanese form and reading.
- Japanese pronunciation plays when the answer is revealed. Use **🔊 Replay**
  or press **R** to hear it again. Speech uses the browser's Japanese
  (`ja-JP`) voice when available and is skipped if speech synthesis is
  unsupported.
- After revealing an answer, use **Keep in Scope & Next** or **Deactivate /
  Memorized & Next**. The keyboard shortcuts are **1** or **→** to keep, and
  **2** or **Enter** to deactivate.
- The sidebar can select cards, enable randomized order, set a card range, and
  independently toggle study status and scope. Progress and order preferences
  are stored in the browser, separately for each deck.

## Creating a deck

Use the `/create-flashcard-deck` workspace prompt in Copilot Chat for a
parameterized deck-generation workflow. It asks for the chapter number, deck
type, and raw card list.

Use [chapter23_words.html](./chapter23_words.html) as the current words-deck
example and [chapter23_kanji.html](./chapter23_kanji.html) as the current
kanji-deck example. The detailed, task-triggered rules and validation checklist
are in
[vocabulary-decks.instructions.md](./.github/instructions/vocabulary-decks.instructions.md).

In short:

1. Add a separate root-level file named `chapter<N>_words.html` or
   `chapter<N>_kanji.html`, based on the matching current example.
2. Keep cards in source order with IDs starting at 1. Preserve supplied
   Japanese forms, readings, and Estonian wording. Do not copy a separate
   English column. Put any clarification to a supplied Estonian meaning in
   square brackets after the original wording.
3. Give every card one matching, original, accessible illustration. Use a
   meaning-matched emoji or inline SVG—not Japanese text as an image or a
   generic fallback icon. Do not hotlink images.
4. Give each deck unique progress and order storage keys. Keep pronunciation,
   replay, keyboard, navigation, responsive, and accessibility behavior
   consistent with the examples.
5. Regenerate the menu with `python generate_index.py`; never edit the
   generated root `index.html` by hand. Validate card data, JavaScript,
   representative rendered cards, and speech behavior before finishing.

## Preview and publishing

Run `python generate_index.py` from the repository root to regenerate the local
menu. The GitHub Pages Actions workflow regenerates the menu in its deployment
artifact and publishes on pushes to `main` that change a root-level HTML file,
`generate_index.py`, or the workflow itself. The committed `index.html` is
useful for local preview; the deployment workflow generates its own copy.

Configure **Settings → Pages → Build and deployment → Source** as
**GitHub Actions**. The published site is
[japanesecatslearning.github.io/japanese](https://japanesecatslearning.github.io/japanese/).
Changes appear online after they are committed to `main`, pushed to GitHub, and
the Pages workflow completes successfully.
